1. `rsft_output_4b_sft_s44/checkpoint-567/` has `adapter_config.json` and `adapter_model.safetensors` — it's a LoRA adapter, not a merged model.

2. `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output/` has adapter files too (adapter_config.json, adapter_model.safetensors) plus checkpoint-144 and checkpoint-96.

Now I need to understand the model loading logic. The test_inference script takes:


- `--model_path`: the RSFT checkpoint (adapter)
- `--base_model`: the base model (Qwen3-4B)
- `--sft_adapter`: the SFT adapter (intermediate step)

But the RSFT checkpoint is itself an adapter, so it should load on top of the base model. Looking at the error log, it tried loading the tokenizer directly from the RSFT checkpoint path, which suggests the code either expected a full model there or attempts to load from the checkpoint first before falling back. I need to check the registry for the test_eval_4b_rsft_s42 experiment specifically and figure out what the correct 4B model setup should be.

Checking the registry file now...

The SFT adapter doesn't have a seed in its name, so this must be the s42 SFT adapter (the default seed). 

The error log shows the command was trying to load from `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output/checkpoint-510`, which is gone, but I actually have everything needed to retrain it — the base model, the SFT adapter, and the RSFT training data are all still there.

Retraining would take about 2 hours based on previous runs, but before I commit to that, let me look at the test_inference.py script to see exactly what's needed. The RSFT model is a LoRA adapter stacked on top of the SFT adapter, which itself sits on the base model, so I need to understand whether I actually need the full RSFT checkpoint or if I can work around this somehow. The task description says the eval just needs to be retried on an idle GPU, but the checkpoint is actually missing, which is a bigger issue. Let me check if I can use the existing `rsft_ced_s43/checkpoint-510` model instead, since it has the same architecture and training as the s42 version I need.

adapter → 新 QLoRA → 在 RSFT 数据上训练。\\n\\n输入: JSONL，每行 {\\\"doc_id\\\": ..., \\\"input\\\": ..., \\\"output\\\": ..., \\\"ced_score\\\": ..., ...}\\n输出: QLoRA adapter checkpoint\\n依赖: transformers, trl, configs/accelerate_4gpu.yaml \\\\\\n            -m freige.training.rsft_trainer --rsft_data_path data.jsonl --output_dir ./rsft_output\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import Dataset\\nfrom peft import LoraConfig, PeftModel, TaskType, get_peft_model\\nfrom torch.utils.data import WeightedRandomSampler\\nfrom transformers import (\\n    AutoModelForCausalLM,\\n    AutoTokenizer,\\n    BitsAndBytesConfig,\\n    TrainerCallback,\\n)\\nfrom trl import SFTConfig, SFTTrainer\\n\\nfrom freige.training import LLAMA3_CHAT_TEMPLATE\\nfrom freige.training.sft_trainer import SYSTEM_PROMPT\\n\\n\\ndef _apply_chat_template(tokenizer, messages, **kwargs):\\n    tmpl = getattr(tokenizer, 'chat_template', None) or ''\\n    if 'enable_thinking' not in tmpl:\\n        kwargs.pop('enable_thinking', None)\\n    return tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, **kwargs)\\n\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nDEFAULT_BASE_MODEL = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B\\\"\\nDEFAULT_SFT_ADAPTER = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_rsft_data(data_path: str, tokenizer, max_length: int = 2048):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"加载 CED 筛选后的 RSFT 数据，格式化为 chat template 文本。\\n\\n    每行 JSONL 的 input 字段作为 user message，output 字段作为 assistant response，\\n    经 apply_chat_template 拼接后用于 SFT 训练。超过 max_length 的样本被跳过\\n    而非截断，避免训练在不完整 JSON 输出上。\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (Dataset, list[float]): 训练数据集和对应的 CED scores\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    samples = []\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                samples.append(json.loads(line))\\n\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d RSFT samples from %s\\\", len(samples), data_path)\\n\\n    formatted = []\\n    ced_scores = []\\n    skipped = 0\\n    for s in samples:\\n        messages = [\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": s[\\\"input\\\"]},\\n            {\\\"role\\\": \\\"assistant\\\", \\\"content\\\": s[\\\"output\\\"]},\\n        ]\\n        text = _apply_chat_template(tokenizer, \\n            messages,\\n            tokenize=False,\\n            add_generation_prompt=False,\\n            enable_thinking=False,\\n        )\\n        token_len = len(tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False))\\n        if token_len > max_length:\\n            skipped += 1\\n            continue\\n        formatted.append({\\\"text\\\": text})\\n        ced_scores.append(s.get(\\\"ced_score\\\", 1.0))\\n\\n    if skipped:\\n        logger.warning(\\\"Skipped %d samples exceeding max_length=%d\\\", skipped, max_length)\\n\\n    if ced_scores:\\n        logger.info(\\n            \\\"Prepared %d RSFT samples (CED scores: min=%.3f, max=%.3f, mean=%.3f)\\\",\\n            len(formatted), min(ced_scores), max(ced_scores),\\n            sum(ced_scores) / len(ced_scores),\\n        )\\n    return Dataset.from_list(formatted), ced_scores\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

## 报告

**原失败原因**：4B RSFT s42 checkpoint (`/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output/checkpoint-510`) 已从服务器删除。`test_inference.py` 尝试从该路径加载 tokenizer/model 失败，回退到 HF Hub 查找也失败（本地路径不是合法 repo id）。

**修复方案**：使用相同训练数据和参数重训 4B RSFT s42。
- 数据：`/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` (2718 CED k=1 样本)
- 参数：bf16 LoRA r=64/α=128, lr=2e-5, 3 epochs, weighted sampling, seed=42
- 输出：`/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s42/` (遵循命名规范)

**当前状态**：
- `retrain_4b_rsft_ced_s42` 在 cuda:0 训练中 (PID 126855)，step 3/510，~7.87s/step，预计 ~66 分钟完成
- 训练完成后需提交 `test_eval_4b_rsft_s42_r2`，使用新 checkpoint 路径 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s42/checkpoint-510`

**下一步**：训练完成后（约 ~1h），提交 test set eval 到空闲 GPU，registry ID `test_eval_4b_rsft_s42_r2`。
